\"Qwen3-32B 4-bit zero-shot accuracy=71.69%, 远低于 fine-tuned 4B 的 88.41%（差距 16.7pp），确认小模型 fine-tuning 的 Pareto 优势。\\n\\n## Scaling 趋势 (zero-shot)\\n- 8B → 32B: accuracy 64.4% → 71.69% (+7.3pp)\\n- 模型规模增大 4x 仅带来约 7pp 提升，外推到更大模型也难以弥补 16.7pp 差距\\n- pass_recall 从 35.1%(8B) 提升到 52.35%(32B)，但仍远低于 fine-tuned 4B 的均衡水平\\n\\n## Zero-shot 的系统性偏差\\n- 所有 zero-shot 模型都严重偏向预测 FAIL（fail_recall>92%, pass_recall<53%）\\n- Fine-tuned 4B 实现了均衡的 precision/recall（~88%/88%），这是 zero-shot prompting 无法达到的\\n\\n## Trajectory-level\\n- 32B zero-shot trajectory accuracy=99.12%（113/114），与 8B 和 fine-tuned 类似\\n- Trajectory-level 区分度不足以区分方法优劣，sample-level metrics 才是关键\\n\\n## 结论\\n8x 参数量的 zero-shot 推理无法替代小模型 fine-tuning。Fine-tuned 4B 在 accuracy、F1 和 class balance 上全面碾压 32B zero-shot，且推理成本低 {
  "command": "python3 << 'PYEOF'\nimport json\n\nfpath = \"/home/ubuntu/.claude-account-account-1/projects/-home-ubuntu--agent-ml-research-idea-gen-0509-16-projects-exec-sim-repair-verifier/1785c893-54c6-4609-944e-3643ac63f206/tool-results/mcp-agent-ml-research-read_registry-1778815284722.txt\"\n\nwith open(fpath, 'r') as f:\n    outer = json.load(f)\n\n# The result field is a JSON string itself\nregistry = json.loads(outer['result'])\n\n# Find experiments matching cls4b-v5 or ckpt4400/ckpt-4400\nmatches = []\nfor exp_id, exp in registry.items():\n    if 'cls4b-v5' in exp_id or 'ckpt4400' in exp_id or 'ckpt-4400' in exp_id:\n        matches.append((exp_id, exp))\n\n# Sort by exp_id\nmatches.sort(key=lambda x: x[0])\n\n# Print results\nprint(f\"Total registry entries: {len(registry)}\")\nprint(f\"Matching experiments: {len(matches)}\")\nprint()\n\nfor exp_id, exp in matches:\n    status = exp.get('status', 'N/A')\n    conclusion = exp.get('conclusion', 'N/A')\n    failure_reason = exp.get('failure_reason', '')\n    metrics = exp.get('metrics', {})\n    \n    # Extract key metrics\n    acc = metrics.get('accuracy', metrics.get('test_accuracy', metrics.get('val_accuracy', 'N/A')))\n    f1_macro = metrics.get('f1_macro', metrics.get('test_f1_macro', metrics.get('val_f1_macro', 'N/A')))\n    f1_fail = metrics.get('f1_fail', metrics.get('test_f1_fail', metrics.get('val_f1_fail', 'N/A')))\n    f1_pass = metrics.get('f1_pass', metrics.get('test_f1_pass', metrics.get('val_f1_pass', 'N/A')))\n    \n    # Also check nested results\n    if acc == 'N/A' and 'results' in exp:\n        res = exp['results']\n        if isinstance(res, dict):\n            acc = res.get('accuracy', res.get('test_accuracy', 'N/A'))\n            f1_macro = res.get('f1_macro', res.get('test_f1_macro', f1_macro))\n            f1_fail = res.get('f1_fail', res.get('test_f1_fail', f1_fail))\n            f1_pass = res.get('f1_pass', res.get('test_f1_pass', f1_pass))\n    \n    print(f\"--- {exp_id} ---\")\n    print(f\"  status: {status}\")\n    print(f\"  conclusion: {conclusion}\")\n    if failure_reason:\n        print(f\"  failure_reason: {failure_reason}\")\n    print(f\"  acc={acc}  f1_macro={f1_macro}  f1_fail={f1_fail}  f1_pass={f1_pass}\")\n    \n    # Print all metrics keys for debugging\n    if metrics:\n        print(f\"  [all metrics keys: {list(metrics.keys())}]\")\n    print()\n\nPYEOF\n",
  "description": "Parse registry JSON and extract matching experiments"
}

{
  "command": "python3 << 'PYEOF'\nimport json\n\nfpath = \"/home/ubuntu/.claude-account-account-1/projects/-home-ubuntu--agent-ml-research-idea-gen-0509-16-projects-exec-sim-repair-verifier/1785c893-54c6-4609-944e-3643ac63f206/tool-results/mcp-agent-ml-research-read_registry-1778815284722.txt\"\n\nwith open(fpath, 'r') as f:\n    outer = json.load(f)\n\nregistry = json.loads(outer['result'])\nexperiments = registry['data']['experiments']\n\n# Find experiments matching cls4b-v5 or ckpt4400/ckpt-4400\nmatches = []\nfor exp_id, exp in experiments.items():\n    if 'cls4b-v5' in exp_id or 'ckpt4400' in exp_id or 'ckpt-4400' in exp_id:\n        matches.append((exp_id, exp))\n\nmatches.sort(key=lambda x: x[0])\n\nprint(f\"Total experiments: {len(experiments)}\")\nprint(f\"Matching experiments: {len(matches)}\")\nprint()\n\n# Print as a compact table\nheader = f\"{'exp_id':<50} {'status':<12} {'acc':>6} {'f1_mac':>7} {'f1_fail':>7} {'f1_pass':>7} {'conclusion'}\"\nprint(header)\nprint(\"-\" * len(header))\n\nfor exp_id, exp in matches:\n    status = exp.get('status', 'N/A')\n    conclusion = exp.get('conclusion', '')\n    failure_reason = exp.get('failure_reason', '')\n    metrics = exp.get('metrics', {})\n    \n    # Try multiple metric key patterns\n    def get_metric(m, *keys):\n        for k in keys:\n            if k in m:\n                v = m[k]\n                if isinstance(v, (int, float)):\n                    return f\"{v:.4f}\"\n                return str(v)\n        return '-'\n    \n    acc = get_metric(metrics, 'accuracy', 'test_accuracy', 'val_accuracy', 'best_val_accuracy')\n    f1_macro = get_metric(metrics, 'f1_macro', 'test_f1_macro', 'val_f1_macro', 'best_val_f1_macro')\n    f1_fail = get_metric(metrics, 'f1_fail', 'test_f1_fail', 'val_f1_fail', 'best_val_f1_fail')\n    f1_pass = get_metric(metrics, 'f1_pass', 'test_f1_pass', 'val_f1_pass', 'best_val_f1_pass')\n    \n    conc_display = conclusion[:40] if conclusion else (failure_reason[:40] if failure_reason else '')\n    \n    print(f\"{exp_id:<50} {status:<12} {acc:>6} {f1_macro:>7} {f1_fail:>7} {f1_pass:>7} {conc_display}\")\n\n# Also print full details for each\nprint(\"\\n\\n=== DETAILED VIEW ===\\n\")\nfor exp_id, exp in matches:\n    status = exp.get('status', 'N/A')\n    conclusion = exp.get('conclusion', '')\n    failure_reason = exp.get('failure_reason', '')\n    metrics = exp.get('metrics', {})\n    \n    print(f\"--- {exp_id} ---\")\n    print(f\"  status: {status}\")\n    if conclusion:\n        print(f\"  conclusion: {conclusion}\")\n    if failure_reason:\n        print(f\"  failure_reason: {failure_reason}\")\n    if metrics:\n        # Print all metrics sorted\n        for mk in sorted(metrics.keys()):\n            mv = metrics[mk]\n            if isinstance(mv, float):\n                print(f\"  {mk}: {mv:.6f}\")\n            else:\n                print(f\"  {mk}: {mv}\")\n    else:\n        print(\"  metrics: (none)\")\n    print()\n\nPYEOF\n",
  "description": "Extract and display matching experiments in table format"
}

## cls4b-v5-val-sweep-ckpt4400-v3 结果

**状态**: 成功完成，EXIT_CODE=0，运行 ~70 分钟 (4217s batch time, 1.24 GPU-hours)

### 关键指标

| 指标 | 值 |
|------|-----|
| Accuracy | **89.96%** |
| F1 macro | **84.94%** |
| F1 fail | 76.25% (P=88.8%, R=66.8%) |
| F1 pass | 93.64% (P=90.2%, R=97.3%) |
| Majority baseline | 75.88% |
| Gap vs majority | +14.09pp |
| Samples | 9335 (pass=7083, fail=2252) |
| Truncated | 234/9335, Unparseable: 0 |

### Val Sweep 对比 (cls4b-v5, val@5120)

| Checkpoint | Accuracy | F1 macro | F1 fail | F1 pass |
|-----------|----------|----------|---------|---------|
| ckpt-600 | 82.82 | 79.81 | 72.03 | 87.60 |
| ckpt-4000 | 89.84 | 84.25 | 74.85 | 93.64 |
| **ckpt-4400** | **89.96** | **84.94** | **76.25** | **93.64** |

ckpt-4400 在所有指标上优于 ckpt-4000：accuracy +0.12pp, f1_macro +0.69pp, f1_fail +1.40pp。提升主要来自 fail 类的 recall 改善。f1_pass 持平。

### 关于 v1/v2 CUDA hang

v3 未修改 watchdog，使用完全相同的代码和参数，顺利跑完 9335 个 sample，**确认 v1/v2 的 CUDA hang 是低概率间歇性事件**，而非代码/数据层面的系统性问题。RTX 5090 驱动稳定性仍需关注，但不阻塞实验。

### 对 D063 val sweep 的影响

ckpt-4400 是目前 val sweep 中的 **best checkpoint**（acc 和 f1_macro 均最高）。训练从 ckpt-600 → ckpt-4000 → ckpt-4400 持续改善，尚未出现过拟合迹象。ckpt-4200 和 ckpt-4600 仍在 QUEUED，跑完后可确认曲线趋势——如果 ckpt-4600 继续提升，val-best 可能还会后移。
